公司簡介
Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company”              舜 宇 光 學 科 技（ 集 團 ）有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」或
or “Sunny Optical Technology”, together with its subsidiaries,              「舜宇光學科技」，連同其附屬公司「本集團」）
the “Group”) (Stock Code: 2382.HK) is a global leading integrated           （股份代號：2382. H K）為一間全球領先並擁
optical components and products manufacturer with more than thirty-          有逾三十五年歷史的綜合光學零件及產品生產
five years of history. The Group is principally engaged in the design,       商 。 本 集 團 主 要 從 事 設 計 、 研 究 與 開 發（「 研
research and development (the “R&D”), manufacture and sales of               發」）、生產及銷售光學及光學相關產品。該等
optical and optical-related products. Such products include optical          產品包括光學零件（例如玻璃球面及非球面鏡
components (such as glass spherical and aspherical lenses, plane             片、平面產品、手機鏡頭、車載鏡頭、安防監
products, handset lens sets, vehicle lens sets, security surveillance        控鏡頭及其他各種鏡頭）        （「光學零件」）、光電
lens sets and other various lens sets) (the “Optical Components”),           產品（例如手機攝像模組、三維（「3D」）光電產
optoelectronic products (such as handset camera modules, three               品、車載模組及其他光電模組）           （「光電產品」）
dimensional (the “3D”) optoelectronic products, vehicle modules and          及光學儀器（例如顯微鏡及智能檢測設備）              （「光
other optoelectronic modules) (the “Optoelectronic Products”) and            學 儀 器 」）。 本 集 團 專 注 於 結 合 光 學 、 電 子 、
optical instruments (such as microscopes and intelligent equipment           軟件及機械技術的光電相關產品的應用領域：
for testing) (the “Optical Instruments”). The Group focuses on the           如手機、數碼相機、車載成像及傳感系統、
application fields of optoelectronic-related products, such as handsets,     安防監控系統及虛擬現實（「VR」）╱ 增強現實

Emergency Response Organizations and Management Groups for                      宿舍、採購、人力資源、財務、監察和宣傳外

overall coordination, temperature measurement, sterilization,                   員實行「員工三色化分類管控和全員網格化管

purposes. The rising of interest rates increases the costs of both              二零二零年十二月三十一日，固定利息銀行借
existing and new debts. As at 31 December 2020, the effective                   貸的實際年利率約2.96%，而可變利息銀行借
interest rate on fixed-rate bank borrowings was approximately 2.96%             貸的實際年利率約0.85%至1.14%。
per annum, while the effective interest rate of variable-rate bank

The Group’s financial assets include derivative financial assets, bank          本集團的金融資產包括衍生金融資產、銀行結

                                                    Management Discussion and Analysis
                                                                                   管理層討論與分析


In order to minimise the credit risk in relation to trade receivables, the   為盡量減低有關貿易應收款項的信貸風險，管
management has delegated a team responsible for the determination            理層已委派專責隊伍，負責釐定信貸限額、審
of credit limits, credit approvals and other monitoring procedures to        批信貸及其他監察程序，以確保採取適當的跟
ensure that appropriate follow-up actions are taken to recover overdue       進行動收回逾期債務。本集團亦已購買有關貿
debts. The Group has also purchased insurances relating to trade             易應收款項的保險。此外，本集團於每個報告
receivables. In addition, the Group reviews the recoverable amount           期期末檢討各項貿易債務的可收回金額，確
of each individual trade debt at the end of each reporting period to         保已為不可收回的金額計提足夠減值虧損。因
ensure that adequate impairment losses are made for irrecoverable            此，董事認為本集團的信貸風險已大大降低。
amounts. Therefore, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk      綜合財務狀況表所呈列金額已扣除呆壞賬撥
is significantly reduced. The amounts presented in the consolidated          備，乃管理層根據過往經驗、對當時經濟環境
statement of financial position are net of allowance for bad and             的評估及將於日後收取的現金流量貼現值估計。
doubtful debts, and are estimated by the management based on their
previous experience, assessment of the current economic environment
and the discounted cash flows to be received in the future.


The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk since its          本集團的貿易應收款項分散於大量交易對手及
trade receivables are dispersed to a large number of counterparties          客戶，故無重大信貸集中風險。由於本集團大
and customers. The credit risk on liquidity is limited because a majority    部分交易對手為獲國際信貸評級機構評定有高
of the counterparties of the Group are banks with high credit-ratings        信貸評級的銀行，故流動資金的信貸風險有限。
given by international credit-rating agencies.


Cash Flow Interest Rate Risk                                                 現金流量利率風險

The Group’s cash flow interest rate risk is primarily related to variable    本集團的現金流量利率風險主要與短期銀行存
rates applicable to short term bank deposits and bank borrowings.            款及銀行借貸的可變利率有關。管理層將檢討
The management will review the proportion of borrowings in fixed and         固定及浮動利率的借貸比例並確保其處於合理
floating rates and ensure they are within reasonable range. Therefore,       範圍內。因此，日後任何利率變化均不會對本
any future variations in interest rates will not have any significant        集團業績造成任何重大影響。
impact on the results of the Group.


Liquidity Risk                                                               流動資金風險

The Group manages liquidity risk by maintaining an adequate level of         本集團持續監察現金流量的預測及實際水平，
cash and cash equivalents through continuously monitoring forecast           並將其與各項金融資產與負債的到期狀況進行
and actual cash flows and matching them with the maturity profiles of        配對，以維持足夠水平的現金及現金等值項
each individual financial assets and liabilities.                            目，從而控制流動資金風險。

THE BOARD                                                                 董事會

Board Functions                                                           董事會職能

Being accountable to the shareholders, the Board is responsible for       董事會須向股東負責，並有責任通過盡責有效
facilitating the further development of the Company’s business by         的方式，推動及領導本公司之業務發展及運
directing and guiding the business developments and operations in a       作，協助本公司業務更進一步發展。董事會成
responsible and effective manner. Board members have a duty to act        員有責任真誠、勤勉及審慎工作，維護本公司
in good faith, with due diligence and care and in the best interests of   及全部股東最佳利益。
the Company and the shareholders as a whole.


While the Company’s business operations are supervised by the             本公司的業務營運由執行董事監察，並由合資
executive Directors and managed by the qualified members of               格管理人員負責管理，董事會負責的決策包
management, the decisions which are to be taken by the Board              括：
include:


1.   Setting the Company’s missions and values;                           1.   訂立本公司目標及價值觀；
2.   Formulating strategic directions of the Company;                     2.   制定本公司策略方針；
3.   Reviewing and guiding corporate strategies;                          3.   檢討及督導企業策略；
4.   Setting performance objectives and monitoring implementation         4.   訂立業績目標及監察實施情況與企業業
     and corporate performance;                                                績；
5.   Monitoring and managing potential conflicts of interest between      5.   監察及管理管理層與董事會成員的潛在利
     the management and Board members;                                         益衝突；

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                                  董事會成員多元化政策
The Board has adopted the following board diversity policy (the         董事會已採納以下董事會成員多元化政策（「董
“Board Diversity Policy”).                                              事會成員多元化政策」）。


Background and Vision                                                   背景與願景

People-oriented and diversity is one of the Company’s core value. The   以人為本及多元化是本公司的核心價值之一。
Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having a diverse        本公司明白並深信董事會成員多元化對提升公
Board to enhance the quality of its performance.                        司表現裨益良多。


Policy Statement                                                        政策聲明

The Company believes that board diversity can enhance                   本公司相信董事會成員多元化能提高決策能
decision-making capability and a diverse board is more effective in     力，而且一個多元化的董事會能更有效地處理
dealing with organisational changes and less likely to suffer from      組織的變化，並可較少受到群體思維的影響。
group thinking. With a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced     為達致可持續及均衡的發展，本公司視提升董
development, the Company sees increasing diversity at the Board level   事會層面的多元化為支持其達到戰略目標及其
as an essential element in supporting the attainment of its strategic   可持續發展的關鍵因素。
objectives and its sustainable development.


In designing the Board’s composition, Board diversity has been          在設定董事會成員組合時，會從不同層面考慮
considered from a wide range of aspects, including but not limited      董事會成員多元化，包括但不限於性別、年
to gender, age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity,         齡、文化及教育背景、種族、專業經驗、營商
professional experience, business perspectives, skills, knowledge and   視野、技能、知識及服務任期，以及董事會不
length of service, and any other factors that the Board may consider    時認為相關及適用的任何其他因素，並著重於
relevant and applicable from time to time. High emphasis is placed      確保董事會成員的技能及經驗組合均衡分佈，
on ensuring a balanced composition of skills and experience for the     以提供不同觀點、見解和提問，讓董事會可以
Board members in order to provide a range of perspectives, insights     有效地履行其職務及職責、就本集團的核心業
and challenges that enable the Board to discharge its duties and        務及策略制定良策，以及配合董事會的繼任計
responsibilities effectively, support good decision making in view of   劃及發展。
the core business and strategy of the Group, and support succession
planning and development of the Board.

Risk Management Policy Framework                                       風險管理政策框架

Effective risk management and internal control systems are critical    有效的風險管理及內部監控系統對實現本集團
in achieving the Group’s strategic objectives. The Group has           的戰略目標至關重要。本集團已制定風險管理
established a risk management policy framework, which is approved      政策框架，由董事會及審核委員會審核通過，
by the Board and the Audit Committee, and executed by the Risk         並由風險管理工作小組執行，授權本集團採用
Management Group. It authorizes the Group to mandates a consistent     貫徹一致及有效的方針，充分管理與戰略、財
and effective approach applied across the Group to fully manage the    務、運營、市場及合規相關的風險。
risks associated with strategies, financing, operations, market and
compliance.

culture.

management and internal control system.

second line of defense.

Phase 1                                                                         第一階段
Goal Setting                                                                    目標設定
The Board establishes common risk assessment criteria and sets up               董事會為本集團設定通用的風險評估準則及制
risk reference tables for the Group. Meanwhile, Risk Management                 定風險參照列表。同時，本集團風險管理工作
Group of the Group and Risk Management Group of each business                   小組和各事業分部風險管理工作小組討論並確
segment discuss and formulate overall risk management objectives,               定風險管理總體目標、管控要求與組織方式。
management requirements and organization methods.


Phase 2                                                                         第二階段
Risk Identification                                                             風險識別
Each business segment and department collects risk information                  各事業分部和部門收集與本集團相關的風險信
related to the Group and identifies the risks that potentially impact           息，並識別對其營運重要程序具有潛在影響的
the key processes of the operations. The Group also identifies                  風險。本集團亦識別改進機會。
opportunities for improvement.

Phase 3                                                                         第三階段
Risk Assessment                                                                 風險評估
Each business segment and department uses uniform and quantitative              各事業分部和部門運用統一及量化的標準，就
criteria to assess the identified risks along with their impacts on the         已識別的風險及其對業務的影響，以及其發生
business and the possibility of their occurrence to determine the risk          的可能性作出評估，確定風險等級。
level.


Phase 4                                                                         第四階段
Risk Response                                                                   風險應對
Each business segment and department determines the overall                     各事業分部和部門對評估出的風險確定總體應
response strategy and corresponding response plan for the assessed              對策略及相應應對計劃，實施風險承擔、風險
risks as well as the implementation measures such as risk taking, risk          規避、風險轉移及風險降低等措施以處理風險。
avoidance, risk transfer and risk reduction to deal with the risks.


Phase 5                                                                         第五階段
Control Activity                                                                控制活動
Each business segment and department establishes or modifies                    各事業分部和部門建立或修訂相應制度和控制
relevant systems and control activity procedures to ensure that                 活動流程，確保採取必要的風險應對和行動計
necessary risk responses and action plans are implemented and the               劃，風險管理應對方案得以執行，以控制和管
risk management response plan is executed, in order to control and              理潛在風險。
manage potential risks.

Phase 6                                                                         第六階段
Monitoring                                                                      監控
Each business segment and department continues to follow up the                 各事業分部和部門持續跟進 KRI 運行情況和風
operation situation of KRI, implementation and effectiveness of the risk        險應對計劃的實施情況及有效性，及時作出風
response plan, makes timely risk warning, adjusts response plan and             險預警，調整應對計劃，並定期向本集團內相
reports risks regularly to the relevant management within the Group.            關管理層匯報風險。內部審計部每年獨立審閱
The internal audit department independently reviews the adequacy and            風險管理體系建設的充足性及有效性，把審閱
effectiveness of the framework of risk management system per annum,             結果及改善建議提報至審核委員會。
and submits the review results and recommendations on improvement
to the Audit Committee.

Group’s Risk Report                                                                         集團風險報告

The Group faces various risks and uncertainties. The relevant key risks                     本集團面對各種風險及不確定因素，有關主要
are the risks that the Group must take in order to achieve its strategic                    風險乃本集團為實現其戰略目標而必須承受
objectives. If they are not handled properly, it might bring adverse                        的風險，倘沒有妥善處理，可能會對本集團
impact to the operations of the Group. Thus, a comprehensive risk                           的運營帶來不利影響。全面的風險評估及風險
assessment and risk mitigation measures help ensure these key risks                         減緩措施有助確保主要風險得到適當管理及有
are properly managed and effectively controlled. The followings are                         效控制。以下乃董事會截至二零二零年十二月
the principal potential risks that the Board has identified for the year                    三十一日止年度已識別的主要潛在風險。

RESTRICTED SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                                       限制性股份獎勵計劃

On 22 March 2010 (the “Adoption Date”), the Board has adopted the                                   於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採納日期」），董
Restricted Share Award Scheme. According to the Restricted Share                                    事會採納限制性股份獎勵計劃。根據限制性股
Award Scheme, the Directors, all employees, senior staff, agents                                    份獎勵計劃，本公司及其附屬公司之董事、全
and consultants of the Company and its subsidiaries are entitled                                    體僱員、高級職員、代理及顧問均有權參與是
to participate in this scheme. The purpose of the Restricted Share                                  項計劃。限制性股份獎勵計劃之目的為協助本
Award Scheme is to assist the Company in attracting new staff as                                    公司吸納新人、激勵及挽留現有人才。該計劃
well as motivating and retaining its current talents. The Restricted                                由採納日期起生效，並持續生效十年，由該計
Share Award Scheme shall be effective from the Adoption Date and                                    劃之管理委員會及受託人管理。於二零二零年
shall continue in full force and effect for a term of 10 years and be                               三月十六日，董事會決議將限制性股份獎勵計
managed by its administrative committee and the trustee. On 16                                      劃的期限延長十年。因此，本應於二零二零年
March 2020, the Board resolved to extend the Restricted Share Award                                 三月二十一日終止的限制性股份獎勵計劃現將
Scheme period for ten years. As a result, the Restricted Share Awards                               於二零三零年三月二十一日終止，除非經董事
Scheme, which shall originally terminate on 21 March 2020, shall now,                               會決議案提前終止。限制性股份獎勵計劃之詳
unless terminated earlier by a resolution of the Board, terminate on 21                             情載於綜合財務報表附註42。
March 2030. Details of the Restricted Share Award Scheme could be
found in the Note 42 to the consolidated financial statements.

1. GENERAL INFORMATION                                               1. 概況資料

 Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the                舜宇光學科技（集團）有限公司（「本公司」）
 “Company”) was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 21              於二零零六年九月二十一日在開曼群島根
 September 2006 as an exempted company under the Companies            據開曼群島公司法第 21 章（一九六一年第
 Law Chapter 21 (Law 3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised) of        三條法例，經綜合及修訂）註冊成立為獲豁
 the Cayman Islands and its shares have been listed on the Hong       免公司，其股份自二零零七年六月十五日
 Kong Stock Exchange with effect from 15 June 2007. Its ultimate      起在香港聯交所上市，最終控股及母公司
 holding and parent company is Sun Xu Limited, a private limited      為舜旭有限公司，一間於英屬處女群島註
 company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands. Its ultimate     冊成立的私人有限公司，而最終控股人士
 controlling party is Mr. Wang Wenjian, also a director of the        為王文鑒先生，亦為本公司董事。本公司
 Company. The addresses of the registered office and principal        註冊辦事處及主要營業地點的地址乃於年
 place of business of the Company are disclosed in the Corporate      報公司資料一節披露。
 Information section of the annual report.


 The Company is an investment holding company. The Company            本公司為一間投資控股公司。本公司及其
 and its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”) are principally      附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）主要從事設計、
 engaged in the business of designing, researching and developing,    研究與開發、生產及銷售光學及光學相關
 manufacturing and selling of optical and optical related products    產品與科學儀器。
 and scientific instruments.


 The consolidated financial statements are presented in Renminbi      綜合財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，
 (“RMB”), which is also the functional currency of the Company.       人民幣亦為本公司的功能貨幣。

   to HKFRSs in the current year had no material impact on the                     則對本集團於目前及以往年度的財務狀況
   Group’s financial positions and performance for the current and                 及表現及 ╱ 或該等綜合財務報表所載之披
   prior years and/or on the disclosures set out in these consolidated             露並無重大影響。
   financial statements.

    Basis of consolidation                                                綜合基準


     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial      綜合財務報表包括本公司、本公司所
     statements of the Company and entities controlled by the             控制實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。
     Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the           當本公司擁有以下權力時，則視為擁
     Company:                                                             有投資對象的控制權：


     •   has power over the investee;                                     •     於投資對象擁有權力；


     •   is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its          •     因參與投資對象業務而可能或有權
         involvement with the investee; and                                     獲得可變回報；及


     •   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •     有能力行使其權力影響該等回報。


     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee          倘有事實及情況顯示上述三個控制因
     if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to        素中之一項或多項出現變化，本集團
     one or more of the three elements of control listed above.           將重新評估其是否對投資對象擁有控
                                                                          制權。


     Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains          本集團獲得附屬公司控制權時即對其
     control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses          綜合入賬，而當本集團失去附屬公司
     control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses         控制權時，即不再對其綜合入賬。具
     of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are          體而言，自本集團獲得附屬公司控制
     included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and         權之日計起，直至本集團不再擁有附
     other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains             屬公司控制權之日止，本年度收購或
     control until the date when the Group ceases to control the          出售附屬公司之收入及開支將列入綜
     subsidiary.                                                          合損益及其他全面收益表內。


     Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income           溢利或虧損及其他全面收益各項均歸
     are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the               屬於本公司股東及非控股權益。附屬
     non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of             公司的全面收益總額歸屬於本公司股

     to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the         控股權益產生赤字差額。
     non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.

    Business combinations                                                      業務合併


     Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the                    收購業務採用收購法入賬。業務合併
     acquisition method. The consideration transferred in a                    之轉撥代價按公允值計量，而計算方
     business combination is measured at fair value, which is                  法為本集團所轉讓之資產、本集團對

     Group to the former owners of the acquiree and the equity                 權於收購日期之公允值總額。有關收
     interests issued by the Group in exchange for control of the              購之成本一般於產生時於損益中確認。
     acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised in

      Investments in associates                                                 於聯營公司之投資


      An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant            聯營公司乃本集團對其具有重大影響
      influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in           力之實體。重大影響力指參與投資對
      the financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but          象財務及營運決策之權力，而並非對
      is not control or joint control over those policies.                      該等政策之控制權或共同控制權。


      The results and assets and liabilities of associates are                  聯營公司之業績、資產及負債使用權
      incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using             益會計法計入本綜合財務報表。以權
      the equity method of accounting. The financial statements of              益會計法處理之聯營公司財務報表乃
      associates used for equity accounting purposes are prepared               按與本集團就於類似情況下之交易及
      using uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for               事件所採用者相同之會計政策編製。
      like transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under              根據權益法，於聯營公司之投資最初
      the equity method, an investment in an associate is initially             乃按成本值於綜合財務狀況表確認，
      recognised in the consolidated statement of financial position            並於隨後就確認本集團應佔該聯營公
      at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                  司之損益及其他全面收益而作出調
      share of the profit or loss and other comprehensive income                整。損益及其他綜合收益外的聯營公
      of the associate. Changes in net assets of the associate                  司資產淨值的變動將不會入賬，除非
      other than profit or loss and other comprehensive income are              有關變動導致本集團持有的所有權權
      not accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes                 益發生變更。如本集團應佔聯營公司
      in ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s                 之虧損超過本集團於聯營公司之權益
      share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s interest              （包括實質上構成本集團於聯營公司淨
      in that associate (which includes any long-term interests                 投資之任何長期權益），則本集團會終
      that, in substance, form part of the Group’s net investment in            止確認其應佔之進一步虧損。如本集
      the associate), the Group discontinues recognising its share              團已產生法定或推定責任或代表聯營
      of further losses. Additional losses are recognised only to               公司支付款項，則就額外虧損作出確
      the extent that the Group has incurred legal or constructive              認。
      obligations or made payments on behalf of the associate.

     an associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate,       於聯營公司之投資時，投資成本超出

     is included within the carrying amount of the investment.             評估後本集團應佔可識別資產及負債
     Any excess of the Group’s share of the net fair value of              公允值淨額超逾投資成本，則即時於
     the identifiable assets and liabilities over the cost of the          收購投資期間在損益中確認。
     investment, after reassessment, is recognised immediately in
     profit or loss in the period in which the investment is acquired.


     The Group assesses whether there is an objective evidence             本集團評估有否客觀證據顯示聯營公

     accordance with HKAS 36 as a single asset by comparing                透過比較其可收回金額（使用價值與公
     its recoverable amount (higher of value in use and fair               允值減出售成本之較高者）與賬面值測
     value less costs of disposal) with its carrying amount. Any           試有否減值。任何確認之減值虧損不
     impairment loss recognised is not allocated to any asset,             會分配至任何資產（包括商譽），其屬
     including goodwill, that forms part of the carrying amount            投資賬面值的一部份。倘其後投資的
     of the investment. Any reversal of that impairment loss is            可收回金額增加，則根據香港會計準
     recognised in accordance with HKAS 36 to the extent that the          則第36號確認該減值虧損的撥回。
     recoverable amount of the investment subsequently increases.

     When the Group reduces its ownership interest in an associate         當本集團削減於聯營公司之所有權權
     but the Group continues to use the equity method, the Group           益但繼續使用權益法時，倘以往於其
     reclassifies to profit or loss the proportion of the gain or loss     他全面收益確認有關削減所有權權益
     that had previously been recognised in other comprehensive            之盈虧部份將於出售相關資產或負債
     income relating to that reduction in ownership interest if that       時重新分類至損益，則本集團會將該
     gain or loss would be reclassified to profit or loss on the           盈虧重新分類至損益。
     disposal of the related assets or liabilities.

     associate are recognised in the Group’s consolidated financial        僅在有關聯營公司之權益與本集團無
     statements only to the extent of interests in the associate that      關的情況下，方會在本集團綜合財務
     are not related to the Group.                                         報表確認。


    Revenue from contracts with customers                                  客戶合約收入


     The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance               當（或於）滿足履約義務時，本集團確
     obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or          認收入，即於特定履約義務的相關商
     services underlying the particular performance obligation is          品或服務的控制權轉讓予客戶時確認。
     transferred to the customer.


     A performance obligation represents a good or service (or             履約義務指不同的商品或服務（或一組
     a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of        商品或服務）或一系列不同的商品或大
     distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.           致相同的服務。


     Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised            控制權隨時間轉移，而倘滿足以下其
     over time by reference to the progress towards complete               中一項標準，則收入乃參照完全滿足
     satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of the     相關履約義務的進展情況而隨時間確
     following criteria is met:                                            認：


     •   the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the             •     隨本集團履約，客戶同時取得並耗
         benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the                     用本集團履約所提供的利益；
         Group performs;

      obtained.

      incremental costs to obtain a contract if these costs would               益攤銷，則本集團會應用可行權宜方
      otherwise have been fully amortised to profit or loss within              法支付所有取得合約的增加成本。
      one year.

    Leases                                                               租賃


     Definition of a lease                                               租賃的定義


     A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the     倘合約為換取代價而給予在一段時間
     right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of     內控制可識別資產使用的權利，則該
     time in exchange for consideration.                                 合約是租賃或包含租賃。


     For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date         就於首次應用日期或之後訂立或修改
     of initial application or arising from business combinations,       或由業務合併所引致的合約而言，本
     the Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a              集團根據香港財務報告準則第16 號的
     lease based on the definition under HKFRS 16 at inception,          定 義 於 初 始 、 修 改 或 收 購 日 期（ 倘 適
     modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.              用 ）評 估 該 合 約 是 否 為 租 賃 或 包 含 租
     Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and           賃。除非合約的條款及條件其後出現
     conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.                變動，否則有關合約將不予重新評估。

      Classification and measurement of leases                                  租賃的分類及計量


      Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as                  本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資

      transfer substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to            上將與相關資產所有權相關的所有風

      classified as a finance lease. All other leases are classified as         被分類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃應
      operating leases.                                                         分類為經營租賃。

     lease. Initial direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging     經營租賃時引致之初步直接成本乃加
     an operating lease are added to the carrying amount of the            至租賃資產之賬面值，有關成本於租
     leased asset, and such costs are recognised as an expense             賃期內按直線法確認為開支。
     on a straight-line basis over the lease term.



    Refundable rental deposits                                             可退回租賃按金


     Refundable rental deposits received are accounted for under           已收可退回租賃按金按香港財務報告
     HKFRS 9 and initially measured at fair value. Adjustments to          準則第9 號入賬，並初步按公允值計
     fair value at initial recognition are considered as additional        量。初始確認時對公允值的調整被視
     lease payments from lessees.                                          為承租人的額外租賃付款。


    Lease modification                                                     租賃修改


     Changes in considerations of lease contracts that were not            不屬於原始條款和條件的租賃合約的
     part of the original terms and conditions are accounted for           條款變更將作為租賃修改處理，包括
     as lease modifications, including lease incentives provided           透過諒解或減租提供租賃優惠。
     through forgiveness or reduction of rentals.


     The Group accounts for a modification to an operating lease           本集團自修改生效之日起將經營租賃
     as a new lease from the effective date of the modification,           的變更作為新租賃入賬，並將與原始
     considering any prepaid or accrued lease payments relating            租賃有關的任何預付款或應計租賃付
     to the original lease as part of the lease payments for the new       款作為新租賃的租賃付款的一部份。
     lease.

    Borrowing costs                                                        借貸成本


     Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,             因收購、興建或生產合資格資產（即需
     construction or production of qualifying assets, which are            相當長時間方可作擬定用途或出售的

     ready for their intended use or sale.


     All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss in the     所有其他借貸成本均於產生期間於損
     period in which they are incurred.                                    益中確認。

     reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the              遵守附帶的條件及收取補助前，均不
     conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be              會確認。
     received.


     Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                 政府補助於本集團確認該等補助擬作
     a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                補償之有關成本為開支之期間內按系
     recognises as expenses the related costs for which the                統基準在損益中確認。尤其是，以本
     grants are intended to compensate. Specifically, government           集團應收購、建造或以其他方式取得
     grants whose primary condition is that the Group should               非流動資產為主要條件的政府補助，
     purchase, construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets           乃於綜合財務狀況表中確認為遞延收
     are recognised as deferred income in the consolidated                 入，並按系統及合理基準於有關資產
     statement of financial position and transferred to profit or loss     的可用年期內轉至損益。
     on a systematic and rational basis over the useful lives of the
     related assets.


     Government grants related to income that are receivable as            作為已產生開支或虧損之補償或向本
     compensation for expenses or losses already incurred or for           集團提供即時財務支持而無日後相關
     the purpose of giving immediate financial support to the Group        成本而應收與收入有關之政府補助，
     with no future related costs are recognised in profit or loss in      於其應收取期間在損益中確認。此補
     the period in which they become receivable. Such grants are           助呈列於「其他收益」項下。

      Taxation                                                                  稅項


      Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                所得稅開支指即期應付稅項與遞延稅
      payable and deferred tax.                                                 項的總和。


      The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the              即期應付稅項根據本年度應課稅溢利
      year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported           計算。應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他
      in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other                 全面收益表所報告的除稅前溢利不
      comprehensive income because of income or expense that                    同，此乃因其他年度的應課稅或可扣
      are taxable or deductible in other years and items that are               稅收支項目，以及毋須課稅或不可扣
      never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current            稅項目所致。本集團的即期稅項負債
      tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or               根據截至報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈
      substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                 的稅率計算。


      Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences
      between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the             遞延稅項按綜合財務報表中資產及負
      consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax               債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相
      bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax             應稅基之暫時差額確認。遞延稅項負
      liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary            債一般就所有應課稅暫時差額確認入
      differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for             賬。遞延稅項資產一般就所有暫時差
      all temporary differences to the extent that it is probable that          額確認入賬，惟可能有應課稅溢利用
      taxable profits will be available against which those deductible          於對銷可扣稅暫時差額。若於一項交
      temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax                  易 中 ， 因 初 次 確 認 資 產 及 負 債（ 業 務
      assets and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary                合併除外）而引致之暫時差額既不影響
      difference arises from the initial recognition (other than in a           應課稅溢利亦不影響會計溢利，則不
      business combination) of assets and liabilities in a transaction          會確認該等遞延稅項資產及負債。此
      that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting                外，倘初步確認商譽產生暫時差額，
      profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if       遞延稅項負債不會確認。
      the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of
      goodwill.

     Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset when there is        當有合法可強制執行權利將與同一稅
     legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets against     務機關徵收之所得稅有關的即期稅項
     current tax liabilities and when they relate to income taxes        資產與即期稅項負債抵銷，則遞延稅
     levied to the same taxable entity by the same taxation              項資產及負債可互相對銷。
     authority.


     Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss,          即期及遞延稅項於損益確認，惟當其
     except when they relate to items that are recognised in other       與在其他全面收益中確認或直接在權
     comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case,          益中確認之項目相關，則即期及遞延
     the current and deferred tax are also recognised in other           稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於
     comprehensive income or directly in equity respectively. Where      權益中確認。倘因業務合併之初步會
     current tax or deferred tax arises from the initial accounting      計方法而產生即期或遞延稅項，有關
     for a business combination, the tax effect is included in the       稅務影響會計入業務合併之會計方法
     accounting for the business combination.                            內。


    Property, plant and equipment                                        物業、機器及設備

     held for use in the production or supply of goods or services,      貨品或服務或作行政用途的租賃土地

     progress, stated in the consolidated statement of financial         乃按成本減其後累計折舊及後續累計
     position at cost less subsequent accumulated depreciation           減值虧損（倘有）於綜合財務狀況表中
     and subsequent accumulated impairment losses, if any.               列賬。


     Properties in the course of construction for production,            用於生產、供應貨品或作行政用途的
     supply or administrative purposes are carried at cost, less         在建物業以成本減任何已確認的減值
     any recognised impairment loss. Costs include any costs             虧損列賬。成本包括導致資產處於對
     directly attributable to bringing the assets to the location        於以管理層擬定之方式營運屬必要的
     and condition necessary for it to be capable of operating           情形及條件而間接產生的任何成本及
     in the manner intended by management and, for qualifying            （ 對 合 資 格 資 產 而 言 ）根 據 本 集 團 會
     assets, borrowing costs capitalised in accordance with the          計政策貨本化的借款成本。該等物業
     Group’s accounting policy. Such properties are classified to        於完成且可作擬定用途時，會列作物
     the appropriate categories of property, plant and equipment         業、機器及設備之適當類別。該等資
     when completed and ready for intended use. Depreciation             產按與其他物業資產相同之方式，於

     of cash-generating units) in prior years. A reversal of an           虧損撥回即時於損益中確認。
     impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating              實際利率法為計算金融資產或金融負
     the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability        債的攤銷成本以及於相關期間內分配
     and of allocating interest income and interest expense over           利息收入及利息支出的方法。實際利
     the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate          率指在金融資產或金融負債的預計年
     that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and             期內或（如適用）較短期間內準確折算
     payments (including all fees and points paid or received that         估 計 未 來 現 金 收 入 及 支 出（ 包 括 所 有
     form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction     構成實際利率整體部份而支付或收取
     costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected           的費用及時點、交易成本及其他溢價
     life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where         或折扣）至初步確認時的賬面淨值的利
     appropriate, a shorter period to the net carrying amount on           率。
     initial recognition.

      Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all                        全期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具預期

      the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from                      期信貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）

      reporting date. Assessment are done based on the Group’s                         發生的違約事件而導致的部份全期預
      historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors that are                 期信貸虧損。預期信貸虧損根據本集
      specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an                      團過往信貸虧損經驗進行評估，並根
      assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting                       據應收賬款特定因素、一般經濟狀況
      date as well as the forecast of future conditions.                               及於報告日期對當前狀況及未來狀況
                                                                                       預測的評估而作出調整。

    (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                                (i)   信貸風險大幅上升


          In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                       於評估信貸風險自初步確認以來有
          significantly since initial recognition, the Group compares              否大幅上升時，本集團比較於報告
          the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument              日期的金融工具違約風險及初步確
          as at the reporting date with the risk of a default                      認日期的金融工具違約風險。作出
          occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of                  評估時，本集團考慮合理且有可靠
          initial recognition. In making this assessment, the Group                資料證明的定量和定性資料，包括
          considers both quantitative and qualitative information                  過往經驗及無需付出過多成本或工
          that is reasonable and supportable, including historical                 作下可獲取的前瞻性資料。
          experience and forward-looking information that is

           an event of default occurs when information developed                  認為違約乃於內部建立或外部來源
           internally or obtained from external sources indicates that            獲取的資料顯示債務人不大可能向
           the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors, including the             債權人（包括本集團）全額結清欠
           Group, in full (without taking into account any collaterals            款時發生（並無考慮任何本集團持
           held by the Group).                                                    有的抵押品）。

           lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                         準。

                 event;                                                              行為；

         financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of        本集團則撇銷該金融資產（例如於
         recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been             交易對手被清盤或進入破產程序
         placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy           時）。金融資產撇銷仍受限於本集

      (v) Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


           The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                計量預期信貸虧損時，會綜合考慮
           of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the              違約可能性、違約損失率（即發生
           loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.               違約時的損失程度）及違約風險承
           The assessment of the probability of default and loss                  擔。違約可能性及違約損失率的評
           given default is based on historical data adjusted and                 定乃基於經調整歷史數據及前瞻性
           forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                資料。預期信貸虧損的估算乃公正
           an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                    及概率加權的數額，其按相應違約
           determined with the respective risks of default occurring              風險的權重釐定。本集團經考慮過
           as the weights. The Group uses a practical expedient in                往信貸虧損經驗後使用撥備矩陣並
           estimating ECL on trade receivables using a provision                  採用實際權益法估計貿易應收款項
           matrix taking into consideration historical credit loss                的預期信貸虧損，並按無需花費不
           experience, adjusted for forward looking information that              必要成本或精力可取得的前瞻性資
           is available without undue cost or effort.                             料調整。


           Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                       一般而言，預期信貸虧損估計為根
           contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                    據合約應付本集團的所有合約現金
           accordance with the contract and the cash flows that                   流量與本集團預期將收取的全部現
           the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective              金流量之間的差額，並按初步確認
           interest rate determined at initial recognition.                       時釐定的實際利率貼現。


           Lifetime ECL for certain trade receivables is considered               若干貿易應收款項的全期預期信貸
           on a collective basis taking into consideration past due               虧損經考慮過往逾期資料及前瞻性
           information and relevant credit information such as                    宏觀經濟資料等相關信貸資料按集
           forward looking macroeconomic information.                             體基準考慮。

      FVTOCI, the cumulative gain or loss previously accumulated in             益工具投資時，先前於按公允值計入

  described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required to        公司董事須就未能於其他來源取得之資產

  amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent          等估計及有關假設乃根據過往經驗及被視
  from other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions             為相關之其他因素而作出。實際結果或有
  are based on historical experience and other factors that are            別於該等估計。
  considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from these
  estimates.


  The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an on-          估計及相關假設將受到持續檢討。倘會計
  going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in         估計修訂只影響該期間，則有關會計估計
  the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision affects      的修訂會在修訂估計期間確認，或倘有關
  only that period, or in the period of the revision and future periods    修訂既影響當前及未來期間，則有關修訂
  if the revision affects both current and future periods.                 會在修訂期間及未來期間確認。

 Provision of ECL for trade receivables                                  貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損撥備

 The Group uses provision matrix to calculate ECL for the trade          本集團使用撥備矩陣以計算並無出現信貸
 receivables which are not credit-impaired. The provision matrix         減值的貿易應收款項的預期信貸虧損。撥
 are based on debtor’s aging as groupings of various debtors that        備矩陣乃基於債務人按各債務人類似虧損
 have similar loss patterns. The provision rate is based on the          模式將其分組的賬齡。撥備率乃基於本集
 Group’s historical observed default rates taking into consideration     團過往觀察的逾期率，並考慮無需付出過
 the forward-looking information that is reasonable and supportable      多成本或工作即可取的合理及可支持的前
 available without undue costs or effort. At every reporting date,       瞻性資料。於每個報告日期，過往觀察的
 the historical observed default rates are reassessed and changes        違約率會重新評估，並會考慮前瞻性資料
 in the forward-looking information are considered. In addition,         的變動。此外，出現信貸減值的貿易應收
 trade receivables with credit impaired are assessed for ECL             款項的預期信貸虧損將獨立地評估。
 individually.


 The provision of ECL is sensitive to changes in estimates. The          預期信貸虧損撥備對估算變動敏感。有關
 information about the ECL and the Group’s trade receivables are         預期信貸虧損及本集團貿易應收款項的資
 disclosed in Note 36(b) and Note 25 respectively.                       料分別於附註36(b)及附註25披露。

   The accounting policies of the operating segments are the same              營運分部的會計政策與附註3所述的本集團
   as the Group’s accounting policies described in Note 3. Segment             會計政策相同。分部溢利指由各分部所賺
   profit represents the profit earned by each segment without                 取的溢利，但並無攤分中央行政成本（包括
   allocation of central administration costs including directors’             董事薪金、其他收益、其他收益及虧損、
   salaries, other income, other gains and losses, share of results of         分 佔 聯 營 公 司 業 績 及 融 資 成 本 ）。 營 運 分
   associates and finance costs. There were asymmetrical allocations           部間存在不對稱分配，這是由於本集團在
   to operating segments because the Group allocates interest                  分配利息收入、政府補助金、折舊及攤銷
   income, government grants, depreciation and amortisation and                以及出售物業、機器及設備收益或虧損至
   gain or loss on disposal of property, plant and equipment to each           各分部時，並未向各分部分配相關銀行結
   segment without allocating the related bank balances, deferred              餘、遞延收入、物業、機器及設備以及無
   income, property, plant and equipment and intangible assets to              形資產。此乃向董事會報告以作資源分配
   those segments. This is the measure reported to the Board of                及表現評估的基準。
   Directors for the purposes of resource allocation and performance
   assessment.

  to transfer goods to customers. All the contract liabilities at the    所有合約負債對應的履約義務均於報告期
  beginning of the period have been realised to revenue in the           內完成，收入得以確認，且本報告期確認

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                   35. 資本風險管理

   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group          本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團轄下實
   will be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the            體能夠持續經營，同時通過優化債務及權
   return to shareholders through the optimisation of the debt and             益結構，實現股東回報最大化。本集團的
   equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged              整體策略與去年相同。
   from prior year.


   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which              本集團的資本架構由負債淨額（包括於附註
   includes the bank borrowings disclosed in Note 30 and bonds                 30中披露的銀行借貸及於附註33中披露的
   payable disclosed in Note 33, net of cash and cash equivalents              應付債券（扣除現金及現金等值項目））及
   and equity attributable to owners of the Company, comprising                本公司股東應佔權益（包括已發行股本、保
   issued share capital, retained profits and other reserves.                  留盈利及其他儲備）組成。


   The directors of the Company review the capital structure on a              本公司董事每半年檢討一次資本架構。作
   semi-annual basis. As part of this review, the directors of the             為檢討的一部份，本公司董事考慮資本成
   Company consider the cost of capital and the risks associated               本及各類資本相關風險。根據本公司董事
   with each class of capital. Based on recommendations of the                 的建議，本集團將透過支付股息、發行新
   directors of the Company, the Group will balance its overall capital        股及股份購回以及發行新債或償還現有債
   structure through the payment of dividends, new share issues                務來平衡其整體資本架構。
   and share buy-backs as well as the issue of new debts or the
   repayment of existing debts.

    Sensitivity analysis                                                         敏感度分析


    The Group is mainly exposed to fluctuation in USD, HKD, JPY,                 本集團的主要風險來自美元、港元、
    INR and VND against RMB.                                                     日元、印度盧比及越南盾兌人民幣的

    The following table details the Group’s sensitivity to a 5%                  下表詳述本集團於各外幣兌人民幣
    (2019: 5%) change in respective foreign currencies against                   升 ╱ 貶值5%（二零一九年：5%）時的
    RMB. The sensitivity analysis includes only outstanding foreign              敏感度。敏感度分析僅包括尚未結算
    currencies denominated monetary items and adjusts their                      的外幣計值貨幣項目，以及有關換算
    translation at the year end date for a 5% change in foreign                  因年結日匯率增減5%而作出的調整。
    currency rates.


    A positive (negative) number below indicates an increase                     下列正數（負數）代表各功能貨幣兌相
    (decrease) in post-tax profit where the respective functional                關外幣升值5%而導致除稅後溢利增加
    currencies strengthen 5% against the relevant foreign                       （減少）情況。倘各功能貨幣兌相關貨
    currencies. For a 5% weakening of the respective functional                  幣貶值5%，則會對除稅後溢利有等額

  c. F a i r v a l u e m e a s u r e m e n t s o f f i n a n c i a l                                        c. 金融工具的公允值計量
     instruments

      This note provides information about how the Group                                                          本附註提供有關本集團如何釐定不同
      determines fair values of various financial assets and financial                                            金融資產及金融負債公允值的資料。
      liabilities.


      The directors of the Company consider that the carrying                                                     本公司董事認為，在綜合財務報表中
      amounts of financial assets and financial liabilities measured                                              按攤銷成本計量的金融資產及金融負
      at amortised cost in the consolidated financial statements                                                  債的賬面值與其公允值相若。

 The Company adopted The Sunny Optical Technology (Group)                本公司已於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採

 a duration of 10 years commencing from the Adoption Date. The           勵計劃」），該計劃自採納日期起持續十年
 objective of the Restricted Share Award Scheme is to provide            有效。限制性股份獎勵計劃旨在為特選參
 the selected participants including directors of the Company,           與者提供（包括本公司董事、僱員、本公司
 employees, agents or consultants of the Company and its                 及其附屬公司的代理或顧問）

 subsidiaries (the “Selected Participants”) with an opportunity to       者」）一個購買本公司所有人權益的機會；
 acquire a proprietary interest in the Company; to encourage and         鼓勵及挽留於本集團工作的個別人士；及
 retain such individuals to work with the Group; and to provide          額外推動彼等爭取達到業績目標，以實現
 additional incentive for them to achieve performance goals, with        提升本公司價值，並且透過擁有股份，使
 a view to achieving the objectives of increasing the value of the       特選參與者的權益與本公司股東的權益直
 Company and aligning the interests of the Selected Participants         接掛鈎的目標。本集團已成立舜宇光學科
 directly to be the owners of the Company through ownership of           技（集團）有限公司限制性股份獎勵計劃信
 shares. The Group has set up The Sunny Optical Technology               託（「信託」）以管理及持有本公司股份直至
 (Group) Company Limited Restricted Share Award Scheme Trust             股份歸屬及轉讓予特選參與者。在向特選
 (the “Trust”) to administrate and hold the Company’s shares before      參與者授出股份時，信託在公開市場上購
 they are vested and transferred to Selected Participants. Upon          買將予授出之本公司股份，並由本公司以
 granting of shares to Selected Participants, the Trust purchases        供款方式提供資金。根據限制性股份獎勵
 the Company’s shares being awarded from the open market                 計劃授出的限制性股份由授出日期第一週
 with funds provided by the Company by way of contributions.             年當日起計至第三到第五（視情況而定）個

 to one-fifth each (as the case may be) on every anniversary date        份無償轉讓予特選參與者，惟特選參與者
